Guido van Rossum pushed zero commits to the python/peps repository on GitHub, triggering an automated activity notification. No PEP, proposal, or technical change was included in the push. The event is routine infrastructure activity rather than substantive technology news.

github · gvanrossum · Sep 21, 17:31

**「Background」** The python/peps repository hosts Python Enhancement Proposals, which document design changes and standards for the Python language. GitHub notifications can include zero-commit pushes when maintainers perform repository operations that update metadata without adding code or documentation.

**「Impact」** There is no immediate impact on Python developers, users, or ecosystem tooling because no technical content was proposed or changed. The notification is informative only and does not affect development workflows or Python standards.

<a id="item-tech-news-4"></a>
### [China disciplines academics after online sleuth exposes misconduct](https://www.nature.com/articles/d41586-026-02890-x) ⭐️ 3.0/10

Disciplinary measures have been imposed on several Chinese scientists investigated for research-integrity violations by the online investigator known as video-blogger &\#x27;Student Geng,&\#x27; according to a Nature report published on 21 September 2026. These actions are part of a broader crackdown involving 31 research teams. The case underscores how citizen-led scrutiny is increasingly influencing official academic oversight in China.

rss · Nature 自然 · Sep 21, 00:00

**「Background」** Research integrity violations include practices such as data fabrication, plagiarism, or image manipulation that undermine the reliability of scientific findings. In China, the government has periodically launched campaigns to crack down on misconduct, while independent online investigators sometimes bring allegations to public attention before formal reviews begin.

**「Impact」** The punished academics face career and institutional consequences, and the episode may encourage further public monitoring of Chinese research practices. It is unclear whether the crackdown will lead to systemic reforms in how allegations are investigated or whether it remains focused on selected high-profile cases.

### [Nature Medicine publishes consensus guideline for personalized bacteriophage therapy](https://www.nature.com/articles/s41591-026-04654-6) ⭐️ 3.0/10

A consensus-based clinical guideline for personalized bacteriophage therapy has been published online in Nature Medicine on 21 September 2026 \(doi:10.1038/s41591-026-04654-6\). The guideline offers practice-oriented recommendations aimed at standardizing the safe clinical use of personalized phage treatments. It represents an effort to create consistent protocols for a therapeutic approach that remains largely individualized outside formal guidelines.

rss · Nature Medicine 自然医学 · Sep 21, 00:00

**「Background」** Bacteriophage therapy involves using viruses that infect bacteria as targeted treatments for bacterial infections, particularly those resistant to conventional antibiotics. Personalized phage therapy tailors these viral treatments to the specific pathogen and patient, but its clinical application has lacked standardized guidelines, leading to variability in practice and safety oversight.

**「Impact」** The guideline provides clinicians with a standardized framework for safely administering personalized bacteriophage therapy in clinical settings. Its adoption may reduce practice variability and improve patient safety as phage therapy moves closer to routine clinical use for antibiotic-resistant infections.

<a id="item-tech-news-156"></a>
### [Scientists&\#x27; posters appear on city commercial screens in China](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182146.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Beginning in September 2026, commercial billboards and LED screens in several Chinese cities, including Heze and Zibo in Shandong, Hulunbuir in Inner Mongolia, Hefei in Anhui, and Hangzhou in Zhejiang, have begun displaying large-scale portraits of prominent scientists such as Qian Xuesen, Deng Jiaxian, Yuan Longping, and Yu Min. The People&\#x27;s Daily commentary highlights this trend as a grassroots effort by mall operators to prioritize public value over commercial advertising, bringing figures known from textbooks into everyday urban spaces. The article frames these displays within a broader movement to promote the spirit of scientists, citing parallel initiatives like lecture teams in Beijing, themed metro trains in Jinan, and educational talks in Shenyang and Dalian. This shift reflects a strategy to make scientific dedication and national service more visible and relatable to the general public, especially younger generations.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Background」** China has a long tradition of honoring scientific contributions to national development, with figures like the &\#x27;Two Bombs, One Satellite&\#x27; engineers and hybrid rice pioneer Yuan Longping serving as iconic role models. The &\#x27;scientist spirit&\#x27;—emphasizing patriotism, innovation, truth-seeking, dedication, collaboration, and mentorship—has been actively promoted through state media and educational channels to inspire future STEM careers and reinforce national pride in technological achievement.

**「Impact」** The placement of scientist portraits on high-traffic commercial screens in multiple provinces increases public visibility of scientific role models in daily life, potentially influencing youth career aspirations and societal attitudes toward science. The initiative demonstrates how private-sector venues can contribute to national cultural campaigns without direct government mandate.

**「Community Discussion」** No community comments are available for this item.

<a id="item-tech-news-157"></a>
### [China achieves second lunar laser communication breakthrough](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182147.html) ⭐️ ?/10

A Chinese scientific team successfully completed a two-way high-speed laser communication trial between Earth and the Moon, becoming the second country globally to master deep-space laser communication technology. The experiment, led by researchers at the Chinese Academy of Sciences&\#x27; Space Application Engineering and Technology Center, achieved a downlink rate of 100 Mbps and uplink rate of 1.25 Mbps over approximately 400,000 kilometers, enabling an uncompressed 8K lunar panoramic photo to transfer in just 12 seconds compared to 4-5 minutes via traditional microwave. The trial overcame three major technical hurdles: precise beam aiming at ultra-long distances, detecting extremely weak signals that attenuate to just a few photons, and processing high-speed data. This breakthrough marks China&\#x27;s transition from near-Earth orbit laser communications into lunar space, supporting future manned lunar missions and deep-space exploration.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Background」** China&\#x27;s deep space exploration has long relied on microwave communication for data transmission, but this technology suffers from limited bandwidth—a critical bottleneck for returning high-resolution images and scientific data from lunar and deep-space missions. Laser communication offers significantly higher data rates with better security and smaller, lighter equipment, but requires solving extreme precision targeting and signal detection challenges across hundreds of thousands of kilometers. The Chinese team conducted over two years of in-orbit testing after the payload was delivered in December 2023, weathering setbacks including satellite solar panel damage and persistent failure through late 2025 before achieving success.

**「Impact」** This achievement positions China among the first tier of nations capable of interplanetary laser communication, directly supporting planned crewed lunar landings and lunar research station construction by providing a reliable high-bandwidth data relay. Future developments aim to increase communication rates to match international leading standards and enable daytime operation in complex weather conditions, ultimately building a complete Earth-Moon information network rather than isolated point-to-point links.

<a id="item-tech-news-158"></a>
### [Chinese researchers synthesize mesoporous carbons and win Future Science Prize](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182148.html) ⭐️ ?/10

A research team led by Chinese Academy of Sciences academician Zhao Dongyuan has been awarded the 2026 Future Science Prize for Physical Science for three decades of work on mesoporous materials. By developing a &quot;stepwise&quot; method—first self-assembling then polymerizing organic precursors—the team successfully synthesized mesoporous polymers and, through carbonization, mesoporous carbons. These materials, with pore sizes between 2 and 50 nanometers, offer high specific surface areas and ordered channels that accommodate large molecules. Practical applications include using mesoporous carbon as a silicon-host framework to mitigate volume expansion in battery electrodes, and mesoporous–zeolite composite catalysts co-developed with Sinopec that have reached kiloton-scale production for upgrading heavy oil and residuum into high-grade fuels.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Background」** Porous materials have been studied for over a century; zeolite molecular sieves and activated carbon are long-standing examples. Mesoporous materials fill the gap between microporous solids \(pores under 2 nanometers\) and macropores, enabling transport and conversion of larger molecules in catalysis, energy storage, and adsorption.

**「Impact」** The stepwise organic–organic self-assembly pathway expanded mesoporous materials beyond inorganics into polymers and carbons, enabling commercial catalyst production and improved lithium-ion battery silicon anodes. Remaining limitations include lack of atomic-scale precision in current mesoporous synthesis and the need for biocompatible, functional variants in biological systems.

<a id="item-tech-news-340"></a>
### [French Justice Targets Corsica&\#x27;s &\#x27;Criminal Bourgeoisie&\#x27;](https://www.lemonde.fr/societe/article/2026/09/21/en-corse-pour-mieux-lutter-contre-la-mafia-la-justice-veut-s-attaquer-a-la-bourgeoisie-criminelle_6779120_3224.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Experienced magistrates in Corsica are pushing to intensify judicial pressure on the island&\#x27;s economic elites who facilitate the interests of organized crime. The strategy shifts focus from direct mafia operatives to the &quot;criminal bourgeoisie,&quot; a class of business and social figures who relay and protect the interests of the &quot;voyoucratie&quot; \(rule by hoodlums\) within the local economy. This approach aims to dismantle the financial and social infrastructure that sustains mafia operations on the island.

rss · 世界报\(Le Monde\) · Sep 21, 12:00

**「Background」** Corsica has long struggled with organized crime, often described by the French term &quot;voyoucratie,&quot; where criminal networks exert significant influence over political and economic life. The concept of a &quot;criminal bourgeoisie&quot; refers to legitimate-looking business people and professionals who serve as intermediaries, laundering money or providing cover for illicit activities, making them harder to prosecute than street-level gang members.

**「Impact」** This judicial shift could lead to increased investigations and prosecutions of prominent Corsican business figures and professionals accused of colluding with mafia groups. It may also cause significant disruption to local economic sectors complicit in money laundering or criminal protection rackets.

On Monday, Russia claimed to have intercepted 45 Ukrainian drones within a twelve-hour period, according to reporting by Le Monde. The drone attacks resulted in two injuries in the Russian Briansk region, as announced by the area&\#x27;s acting governor. This report is part of an ongoing live feed covering developments in the war in Ukraine.

**「Background」** The conflict between Russia and Ukraine has seen a significant escalation in the use of unmanned aerial systems by both sides for reconnaissance and striking targets deep behind enemy lines. The Briansk region is a border area adjacent to Ukraine that has frequently been cited in reports of cross-border drone activity and sporadic attacks.

**「Impact」** Residents and officials in the Briansk region face ongoing security risks and potential infrastructure damage from these cross-border drone incursions. For military observers, the claimed interception of 45 drones highlights the intensity of aerial warfare and the resources required for regional air defense.

In their performance &quot;Mon frère&quot; at the Théâtre du Rond-Point in Paris, François and Christian Gremaud explore the theme of disability with tenderness and political depth. Christian, who is deaf, shares an intimate narrative that underscores the necessity of resistance for people labeled as non-disabled. The brothers provided an interview to Le Monde detailing how they conceptualized this universally resonant story.

rss · 世界报\(Le Monde\) · Sep 21, 18:00

**「Background」** The Théâtre du Rond-Point is a prominent performing arts venue in Paris known for hosting diverse theatrical productions. The Gremaud brothers are recognized for their collaborative work that often addresses social and personal identities through performance art.

**「Impact」** The production offers a platform for disability representation in mainstream French theater, challenging societal perceptions through personal narrative. It highlights the intersection of artistic expression and political advocacy for the deaf and disabled community.

**「Background」** Saint-Pierre-et-Miquelon is a French overseas collectivity located just 25 kilometres off the coast of Newfoundland, Canada, making it France&\#x27;s only North American territory. The recent meeting between French President Emmanuel Macron and Canadian Prime Minister Mark Carney there marked the first official visit by a Canadian prime minister to the territory, serving as both a diplomatic symbol and a practical venue for discussing closer economic and defense cooperation.

**「Impact」** This strengthened partnership may reshape North Atlantic economic and security dynamics as Canada and France seek alternatives to US-centric trade and European energy dependence.

### [Berlin: Son of Remmo releases alleged chats with Left party&\#x27;s Ferat Koçak](https://www.spiegel.de/politik/deutschland/berlin-remmo-sohn-veroeffentlicht-angebliche-chats-mit-linke-abgeordnetem-ferat-kocak-a-3c56502d-64a1-4908-82be-0e9a9ff15945#ref=rss) ⭐️ ?/10

The son of Berlin-based individual Issa Remmo has released alleged chat messages involving Bundestag member Ferat Koçak from the Left party \(Die Linke\). The publication follows questions about whether Remmo&\#x27;s appearance at a Left party event in Berlin was coincidental, as the party has attempted to downplay the significance of that encounter. According to Der Spiegel, the released conversations present a markedly different picture than the party&\#x27;s public narrative.

rss · 明镜周刊\(Der Spiegel\) · Sep 21, 15:38

**「Background」** Issa Remmo is a prominent Berlin-based figure linked to organized crime, often described in German media as a clan boss. His son released alleged chat messages between Issa Remmo and Ferat Koçak, a Bundestag member of the Left Party \(Die Linke\) representing Neukölln. The publication follows questions about whether Remmo&\#x27;s appearance at a Left Party event in Berlin was incidental, with the party seeking to downplay the connection.

### [Putin&\#x27;s party extends parliamentary majority amid wartime sentiment](https://www.spiegel.de/ausland/russland-putins-sieg-verschleiert-die-schlechte-stimmung-a-5b5a82ca-e84b-415d-a559-519f2a2499a8#ref=rss) ⭐️ ?/10

Russia&\#x27;s ruling party has expanded its constitutional majority in parliament following four and a half years of war, signaling the regime&\#x27;s firm grip on institutional power. According to Der Spiegel, this consolidation says little about genuine public opinion and instead illustrates how entrenched the Kremlin remains politically despite underlying societal discontent. The editorial highlights a contrast between formal political dominance and potentially muted popular sentiment.

rss · 明镜周刊\(Der Spiegel\) · Sep 21, 15:34

**「Background」** Russia&\#x27;s parliamentary elections operate under a system where the ruling United Russia party has long dominated state institutions. A constitutional majority allows the governing party to alter the constitution without opposition support, a threshold it has progressively reinforced since the conflict began in 2022.

**「Impact」** The entrenched parliamentary supermajority enables further constitutional changes without checks from opposition forces. This consolidation may limit domestic political alternatives while external sanctions and wartime conditions continue to shape the broader environment.

### [Berlin election: Elif Eralp victory signals leftward shift](https://www.spiegel.de/politik/deutschland/elif-eralps-wahlsieg-berlin-hat-verstanden-dass-die-zukunft-links-ist-a-3eaf5136-0150-4c14-a1c0-79ba23b6c67f#ref=rss) ⭐️ ?/10

Elif Eralp secured a victory in Berlin&\#x27;s election, reflecting a broader leftward trend in the German capital. The win underscores that policies prioritizing affordable housing and livability resonate more with voters than those favoring wealthy interests, according to Der Spiegel analysis. This result is causing concern among centrist and right-leaning political forces, who see it as a setback for their agenda.

rss · 明镜周刊\(Der Spiegel\) · Sep 21, 15:33

**「Background」** Berlin has long been a stronghold for left-leaning politics in Germany, with housing affordability and social welfare being key electoral issues. Elif Eralp, a candidate aligned with these priorities, capitalized on widespread public concern over rising living costs. The election highlights the growing political influence of urban voter concerns around economic inequality.

**「Impact」** Eralp&\#x27;s victory strengthens the political position of left-wing parties in Germany and may influence national debates on housing and social policy. Centrist parties now face pressure to address affordability concerns or risk further losses in urban areas.
